> **Last updated:** 2026-08-03 — **Playwright E2E promoted from report-only to a HARD CI GATE.** Closes the hole that let the 2026-07-29 blank-product-page P1 through: the suite caught it, but `continue-on-error: true` meant the run still reported green. Removed that line from the `e2e` job in `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, so a failing browser run now fails CI — which is the signal `git ship` waits on (there's no server-side PR gate since the client dropped the ruleset, so the enforcement is still discipline, just with an honest red now). ⚠️ **Verification gotcha worth knowing:** you cannot read "was it passing?" off the workflow-level conclusion — `continue-on-error` makes the *run* report success regardless, which is the whole trap. Check the **step-level** conclusion instead (`gh run view <id> --json jobs --jq '.jobs[] | select(.name=="E2E (Playwright)") | .steps[]'`), which does reflect the real exit code. Confirmed green there on the last 4 runs, then re-ran the suite locally for direct evidence before flipping it: **24/24 passed in 2.2 min**. Also added **`timeout-minutes: 20`** (the default job timeout is 6 hours — fine for a job that can't fail the build, wasteful for one that can hang the gate); flake mitigation was already in place (`retries: 1` under CI in `playwright.config.ts`) and the report upload already runs `if: !cancelled()`, so a red run still yields traces + screenshots. All three workflow files re-validated through a real YAML parse.
>
> **Last updated:** 2026-07-30 — **Guzzle security advisories actually cleared (7.12.3 → 7.15.2).** The 4 medium advisories outstanding since 2026-07-22 are fixed in 7.15.1/7.14.2, and `composer audit` now reports **"No security vulnerability advisories found."** ⚠️ Getting there exposed a trap worth knowing — a plain `composer update guzzlehttp/guzzle` installed a **dev-branch commit** (`7.12.x-dev`) and fixed nothing, because single-package updates don't touch that package's own dependencies and the starter kit allows dev stability; `--with-dependencies` was the fix. Full symptom→cause→fix under **Local Development → Composer gotcha**. Verified: **348 tests / 1570 assertions** still green after the upgrade (every payment/shipping/WhatsApp call goes through Guzzle via Laravel's Http client). _Noted for later: the wider tree is ~32 packages behind (Laravel 12.62→12.64, Inertia 3.1→3.2, Pint, aws-sdk…) — a deliberate maintenance pass of its own, not something to slip into a security fix._
>
> **Last updated:** 2026-07-30 — **Resend wired up, so the email channel is no longer inert.** Installed `resend/resend-php` (the framework's `ResendTransport` needs it); `config/mail.php`/`config/services.php` already had the mailer + `RESEND_KEY` binding, so no config edits. Client's key added to `.env` (gitignored — verified it appears in **no tracked file**). **Verified with a real send** to Resend's simulator address. Three findings worth keeping: (1) **the key is send-only/restricted**, so `GET /domains` returns 401 `restricted_api_key` — new **`App\Support\ResendProbe`** treats that as OK (invalid keys answer 400 `validation_error`, full-access keys 200 + domain statuses) and `integrations:check` now reports email beside the payment providers, including a warning when `MAIL_MAILER` isn't `resend` or the from-address is still the scaffold placeholder; (2) 🔴 **`retabstore.com` is NOT verified** — proven by an actual send failing with *"domain is not verified"*, so DKIM/SPF in Cloudflare DNS is the one remaining step and `onboarding@resend.dev` is the only usable from-address until then; (3) **dev stays on `MAIL_MAILER=log` on purpose** — the seeded staff have fake `@retab.com.sa` addresses and bouncing them off a fresh account would hurt its reputation, so new **`php artisan mail:test <address>`** sends through the resend mailer explicitly instead. `.env.example` documents the whole block. Verified: **348 tests / 1570 assertions** (+6), Pint ✓. _Note: `composer audit` still flags 4 pre-existing **guzzlehttp/guzzle** advisories (not from this change, outstanding since 2026-07-22) — worth a `composer update guzzlehttp/guzzle`._

> **Last updated:** 2026-07-30 — **Notification history page (`/admin/notifications`).** The bell keeps only the latest 10, so the 11th alert was unreachable. Added a paginated own-notifications list with **read-state + type filters** (whitelisted JSON-path where on `data->type`; verified on SQLite **and** MariaDB, and MySQL 8 is stricter-better), reached via a **"View all"** footer link in the dropdown; rows reuse the existing open route so clicking still marks read + redirects to the subject. Payload→text rendering was **extracted to `components/admin/notification-content.tsx`** (`useNotificationText`/`iconFor`/`NOTIFICATION_TYPES`) and both the bell and the page import it, so a new type can't render in one and not the other. ⚠️ **Gotcha: the page paginator is named `entries`, not `notifications`** — that name is the shared bell prop (`{unread, items}`) and a same-named page prop silently shadows it, which would make the bell throw on this very page (regression test pins it). No sidebar entry by design — notifications live on the navbar bell. Verified: **342 tests / 1553 assertions** (+5), `tsc` ✓, `lint` ✓, Pint ✓, and **3/3 admin-notification E2E specs green** including reaching the page through the bell and confirming the type filter empties the list. Full writeup in **Architecture → Notifications & alerts**.

> **Last updated:** 2026-07-30 — **Staff notifications: email channel, queued WhatsApp transport, live bell — plus a P1 product-page crash found while verifying.** Full writeup in the new **Architecture → Notifications & alerts**.
> - 🔴 **Fixed a white-screen on the product page (unrelated to this work, live since 2026-07-29 `c799da2`).** `ShopController::show` shipped the review-reward prop as **`review_reward`** while `shop/product.tsx` destructures **`reviewReward`** → `reviewReward.available` threw during hydration and **every visitor got a blank product page** (HTTP 200, HTML delivered, React dead). _Why nothing caught it:_ an Inertia prop-name mismatch is invisible to `tsc` (untyped boundary) and to PHPUnit status-code assertions, and the **E2E suite that did catch it is `continue-on-error` in CI**, so the run still reported success. _Fixed_ (camelCase, matching `hasOffers`/`turnstileSiteKey`/`lowStockList`) + a regression test pinning **every prop the page destructures** (`ShopControllerTest::test_product_page_ships_every_prop_the_page_destructures`). **Lesson: promote the E2E job to a hard gate** — it was already on the roadmap and would have blocked this.
> - **Email channel** on all three staff alerts (new order / return filed / product requested): `toMail()` + `ShouldQueue`, with mail **conditional on `$notifiable->email`** (nullable under the OTP identity model — the transport throws on an empty address) and **`viaConnections(['database' => 'sync'])`** so the bell row still lands in-request while only the email queues. Emails are deliberately **English literals** (the admin's panel language lives in their browser; a customer-triggered notification can't know it). The app previously sent **zero** business email despite the client brief asking for email alerts.
> - **WhatsApp transport moved onto the queue** — new `SendWhatsappMessage` job (tries 3, backoff 30/120/600s, idempotent). Every transactional send used to be an inline Meta HTTP call **per recipient inside the customer's request**; a slow API added seconds to checkout and a failure lost the alert to a log line. Ledger row still written synchronously as `queued`. Three deliberate subtleties: params are passed to the job (the ledger **redacts** OTP codes, so the row isn't a faithful payload), **OTP stays inline** (customer is waiting + keeps plaintext codes out of the `jobs` table), and the job **rethrows for retries except under the `sync` driver** so a Meta outage can never surface inside a checkout.
> - **The bell is live** — `router.poll(45s, { only: ['notifications'] })`, Inertia's own poller (hidden tab throttled to 1-in-10 ticks), so an order arriving while an admin sits on one page now surfaces without a manual reload. Plus a tab-title unread badge, an optional WebAudio chime (mute toggle persisted per browser) and a `notifications-dropdown` testid. ⚠️ **Gotcha: Inertia owns `<title>`** and rewrites it on hydration/navigation, silently dropping a badge written once (even one `requestAnimationFrame` late) — fixed with a `MutationObserver` on `document.head` + an equality guard against an observer loop.
> - 🔴 **New deploy prerequisite: a `queue:work` service** (see the Notifications section). Not new in spirit — `SendReviewReminder` and `SendWhatsappCampaign` already depended on a worker silently; now admin email + WhatsApp do too. The bell deliberately does not.
> - **Verified:** **337 tests / 1481 assertions** (+9), `tsc` ✓, `lint` ✓, 21 JS ✓, client+SSR builds ✓, and **23 E2E green** including a new `e2e/admin-notifications.spec.ts` that places a real order and asserts it reaches the bell, badges the tab title, and that a partial `notifications` poll fires in a real browser (server log confirmed the second request exactly 45s after the first).

> - **Review reward** (`c799da2`, `1fec321`, `50a142f`) — "write a review, get a discount": a verified buyer who reviews **with a comment** earns a one-time 10%/20% account-bound coupon valid 30 days, via `App\Services\ReviewRewardService` (row-locked, idempotent, once-ever per customer through the `loyalty_rewards` unique key). Off by default; managed on **`/admin/discounts`** (moved there out of Settings, and the page's columns were swapped so tools sit inline-start and current/history inline-end). A `SendReviewReminder` job dispatched with a **1-day delay** from `ShippingService` on delivery sends the WhatsApp nudge, deep-linked to a purchased product's `#reviews`, re-checking eligibility at send time and stamped by `orders.review_reminder_sent_at` (new migration) so a repeated "delivered" webhook can't re-send. Storefront shows the earn-nudge only while still earnable. Full writeup under **Coupons — DECIDED**; 195 lines of tests in `tests/Feature/ReviewRewardTest.php`.

Every ad-hoc status badge in the panel collapsed into one soft-tint `StatusPill` (dotted, dark-mode-safe tones); product visibility + coupon active became **one-click `StatusToggle`s** from the list (no confirm — reversible — with hover hints explaining blocked states); the undo/revert confirm got a styled dialog; the layout-shifting flash banner was replaced by **overlay toasts** (success auto-dismisses, errors linger); the inventory sync banner gained **health states + friendly relative time** (`iso` shipped alongside the exact stamp so the client renders "N days ago" via `Intl.RelativeTimeFormat` in the admin's own language). ⚠️ **The one real gotcha:** toasts needed a **per-response `flash.id` nonce** in `HandleInertiaRequests` — two consecutive actions flashing the *same* text wouldn't re-fire a value-compared effect dep, so the second toast never appeared.

> **Last updated:** 2026-07-26 — **Full security audit + hardening pass.** Swept the whole attack surface (authZ/IDOR, admin permission gating, checkout price integrity, all four webhook signatures, input validation, file upload, rate limits/CSRF/headers/proxies, data exposure, injection, mass assignment). **No Critical/High issues** — the payment layer (re-fetch + row-lock + amount/currency check, tampering logged never fulfilled), webhook signature verification, OTP (hashed/expiry/single-use/5-attempt cap), IDOR gating, and admin user-management are all genuinely solid. Applied six hardening fixes with tests: (1)+(2) added per-IP `throttle` to `login`/`forgot-password`/`reset-password` (login also keeps the per-email+IP `LoginRequest` limiter); (3) **removed `role`+`permissions` from `User::$fillable`** (privilege fields) — set only via `forceCreate`/`forceFill` at the trusted setters (Admin\UserController, OtpAuthController, AdminUserSeeder wrapped in `User::unguarded`); ⚠️ **gotcha:** Eloquent **factories bypass `$fillable`** (verified — `User::factory()->create(['role'=>...])` still works), but non-factory `User::create([...role...])` respects it, so the ~9 test helpers using it were switched to `User::forceCreate`; (4) capped cart `quantity` at `max:99`; (5) `lockForUpdate()` on the coupon row in `CheckoutService::resolveCoupon` (kills the check-then-increment over-redemption race); (6) added the `admin` route middleware to `settings/reset` (belt-and-suspenders with its in-controller `isAdmin` check). New regression tests in `tests/Feature/Security/SecurityHardeningTest.php` (role can't be mass-assigned, profile update can't escalate, cart cap, login + forgot-password rate limits). Deferred (noted, low priority): nonce-based CSP to drop `script-src 'unsafe-inline'`; sanitize `Media` `$dir` against `..`. Verified: **310 tests / 1343 assertions** (5 new), Pint ✓.

To make that fast and verifiable: added a non-throwing **`ping()`** to `MoyasarGateway` / `TamaraClient` / `OtoClient` (each reuses its real auth: Moyasar lists invoices, Tamara reads a bogus order so 404=authed vs 401=rejected, OTO does the refresh-token exchange) + **`php artisan integrations:check`** (`{--offline}`) — per provider it reports env presence, the exact **webhook URL to register** (from APP_URL), and a live auth probe (OK / FAILED / NOT CONFIGURED); exit code fails only when a *configured* provider fails, so it's deploy-smoke-check-safe. > **Last updated:** 2026-07-25 — **Zid 301 redirect map + Arabic slug cleanup (SEO cutover safety).** The Zid import seeded `products.slug` from the old Zid `product_page_url`, and Zid used the same `/products/{slug}` path we do (verified live), so the currently-indexed URLs resolve today — but any slug we clean would 404 and drop its Google ranking. Built (see Architecture → SEO for the full writeup): a **`redirects` table** + `App\Models\Redirect` (301 map, targets the product so it follows the current slug), a **`catalog:clean-slugs {--apply}`** command that regenerates only the **junk** slugs (ar-prefixed / English / uppercase / leading-hyphen) into clean **Arabic** from `name_ar` via **`App\Support\ArabicSlug`** (preserves Arabic + Arabic-Indic digits; Str::slug would transliterate them away), leaving already-clean Arabic slugs untouched (no redirect → fewest 301s → best ranking preservation), and a **`->missing()`** hook on `/products/{product:slug}` → `RedirectController` that 301s old→new (route:cache-safe via SerializableClosure — verified). Client chose Arabic slugs over English. On the real catalogue: **27 of 87** slugs cleaned; applied locally + curl-verified (old English/ar- slug → 301 → Arabic, new slug → 200, garbage → 404). **Launch step:** run `catalog:clean-slugs --apply` on prod once final slugs are settled. Verified: **297 tests / 1318 assertions** (18 new), Pint ✓, `route:cache` ✓.

Next up: fix CI, then merge `construction_phase` → `main` (PR #3 already merged up through the SSR/SEO commit; main is 4 commits behind — marketing, CI + Tajawal, 2 doc commits; pushing `main` will eventually drive the Railway prod deploy), then the Production-deploy checklist.

> **Last updated:** 2026-07-02 — **Returns module built end-to-end (+ real gateway refunds).** Customer flow: `GET/POST /orders/{order}/return` (owner-gated; eligibility = delivered + 3-day window + no open return, enforced in `ReturnService`), photos through `Media` to `returns/{order_id}`, bilingual `shop/return-request` page + status/entry-button on the order page. Admin flow: `/admin/returns` list/detail with photos + a refund preview for both shipping toggles → approve/reject → exchange or refund. **New refund plumbing:** `refundPayment()` added to the `PaymentGateway` contract (Moyasar impl) + `PaymentService::refund` and `TamaraClient/TamaraService::refund`; refund ledger rows record the ACTUAL amount (a direct `Payment::create` — `recordTransaction` assumes order-total, wrong for partials); `payment_status` moves to `refunded`/`partially_refunded` off the summed refund ledger. `OrderConfirmationService::releaseFunds` card-refund TODO retired (real refund, best-effort). WhatsApp `return_update` (customer) + `admin_return_requested` (admins) templates added — remember to create + get them approved in Meta before launch. Verified: **123 tests / 428 assertions** (5 new: filing+photos, window, duplicate-block, ownership, admin refund partial-math) + client & SSR builds.

> **Last updated:** 2026-07-02 — **Killed the recurring Intelephense P1005/P1013 false positives — for the whole machine, not just this repo.** True root cause was **not** Eloquent magic itself: the official Laravel VS Code extension generates `vendor/_laravel_ide/_model_helpers.php` with defaults-less `@method static mixed where($column, $operator, $value, $boolean)` tags that shadow the real signatures, **and** a second embedded Intelephense (the `porifa.laravel-intelephense` fork, no diagnostics settings) kept re-reporting it — which is why the old global `argumentCount: false` never worked. Fixed by uninstalling the fork + excluding `**/vendor/_laravel_ide/**` via global `intelephense.files.exclude` (restored `argumentCount` checking). Kept `barryvdh/laravel-ide-helper` in-repo for correct signatures + model autocomplete (self-heals via `post-update-cmd`). Full gotcha in Local Development. 118 tests still green.

> **Last updated:** 2026-07-02 — **Arabic-first i18n + RTL foundation, then storefront localization (AR⇄EN).**
> - **i18n foundation** (`b94cd6d`): `SetLocale` middleware (default **AR**) + `LocaleController` + `POST /locale/{locale}`; `HandleInertiaRequests` shares `locale`; `<html dir>` set from locale in `app.blade.php` (RTL on first paint, no flash); installed `react-i18next` + `i18next` (runtime deps → survive SSR prune) with `resources/js/i18n/{ar,en}.ts` bundles; `LanguageContext` (session-cookie source of truth, no localStorage) wired into `app.tsx` + `ssr.jsx`; AR⇄EN toggle in the store header.
> - **Storefront localization** (`da3f36f`): all 10 storefront/account/auth pages + `store-layout` converted to `t()` (incl. the order-status map, GCC country names, payment-method labels that were inline Arabic dicts). DB content (product/category names) localized via **`useLocalized()`** — controllers (`ShopController::card`, `CartService::summary`, `WishlistController`) now ship both `_ar`/`_en` and the client picks by locale so the toggle is **instant** (no reload), mirroring Sky Amman. Verified: client build ✓, SSR build ✓, 118 tests / 408 assertions ✓, zero Arabic literals left in converted pages.
> - **Server-side localization** (same-day follow-up): flash/error strings → `__('messages.*')` (`lang/{ar,en}/messages.php`) across storefront + admin controllers/services; Laravel validation/auth/passwords localized (`lang/ar/*` mirroring published `lang/en/*`, with AR `attributes`). 118 tests still green (AR values equal the old literals, so flash assertions hold). ⚠️ Resolves at request time — follows on the next request, not the instant client toggle.
> - **Deferred (distinct mechanisms):** admin-panel UI i18n (still EN/LTR by design), Arabic web font (Tajawal / IBM Plex Sans Arabic), and honoring the session locale in SSR once runtime SSR lands.
> - **Also:** corrected the stale Build Progress checklist — the storefront vertical + much of admin were already built but still showed as TODO.

> - **"Log the tricky stuff" convention** added: document any issue/blocker/non-obvious behavior with symptom → root cause → fix (inline in retab's style and/or a line here) so it isn't re-hit — including in sibling projects on the same stack.

> **Last updated:** 2026-06-28 — **Scaffold + CLAUDE.md, Inertia v2 → v3, local DB → XAMPP MariaDB, dual-push to client repo wired.**
> - **Dual-push to client repo configured:** added `https://github.com/retab-dates-dev/retab-website.git` as a 2nd push URL on `origin` (now 1 fetch + 2 push URLs) — this is the **Railway production source**. Read access verified (`ls-remote` exit 0); repo is empty so first push will populate it. Chose dual-push (one `git push` → both repos) over a separate `client` remote.
> - **Local DB configured + migrated:** `.env` points at XAMPP **MariaDB 10.4.32** (`127.0.0.1:3307`, db `retab-stores`, root / empty password); ran `php artisan migrate` (users/cache/jobs + sessions tables created). Confirmed the engine is **MariaDB**, not MySQL — XAMPP ships MariaDB under the "mysql" name (binary `C:\xampp\mysql\bin\mysqld.exe`, service `mysql`); TablePlus was just the client used to create the DB. `APP_KEY` already present from scaffold. Flagged dev↔prod DB parity (prod planned MySQL 8) as a pending decision.
> - **Inertia v2 → v3 upgrade** (on branch `construction_phase`, before any feature work): `inertia-laravel` v2.0.24→v3.1.0, `@inertiajs/react` ^2.0→^3.0 (3.5.0). Verified the doc's "v2 vs v3" framing against both lockfiles (Sky Amman really is v3.0.4) and confirmed v3 needs only PHP 8.2 — the PHP-8.3 pin was about the scaffolding template, not Inertia. Only the scaffold's `resolvePageComponent` resolve needed changing (v3 dropped default-export auto-unwrap → added `.then(m => m.default)` in `app.tsx`/`ssr.jsx`, mirroring Sky Amman). Build + 26 tests green. This resolves the former "Inertia is v2 here" divergence from Sky Amman.
> - Scaffolded `retab-stores` with the official Laravel 12 React starter kit (Inertia v2 + React 19 + TS + Tailwind v4, PHP 8.2 / starter kit v1.0.1). Added `.npmrc` before install to dodge the `NODE_ENV=production` devDep trap; production build verified clean. Git on `main`, `origin` → `mercenary19961/retab-stores` (dual-push to the client repo to be added once it exists).
